Conclusion: 通过结合位向量与整数算术推理，并采用可靠的跨理论信息交换，Mosaic提升了约束Horn子句在定长位向量理论下的求解效率，为可扩展的位精确程序验证提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Deciding satisfiability of Constrained Horn Clauses (CHCs) modulo the theory of fixed-size bit-vectors ($\mathcal{T}_B$) is fundamental to bit-precise program verification. However, state-of-the-art CHC-solvers often struggle with $\mathcal{T}_B$, limiting scalability in bit-precise reasoning. We present Mosaic, a theory-modular framework for deciding satisfiability of CHCs modulo $\mathcal{T}_B$ by combining reasoning in $\mathcal{T}_B$ and the theory of Integer Arithmetic ($\mathcal{T}_I$). Given a CHC set modulo $\mathcal{T}_B$, Mosaic partitions it into two fragments interpreted over $\mathcal{T}_B$ and $\mathcal{T}_I$. Moreover, it implements an algorithm that reasons about the fragments in a modular fashion, exchanges information between them via sound translations across theories, and determines satisfiability w.r.t. the original CHC set. We implemented a prototype of Mosaic using Z3 and Spacer and evaluated it on bit-manipulating benchmarks. Our evaluation shows that Mosaic significantly outperforms Spacer on these benchmarks.

</details>


### [127] [Can Open-Weight LLMs Produce Kernel-Verified Coq Proofs? A Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05420)
*Ahmed Ryan,Md Erfan,Akond Ashfaque Ur Rahman,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本研究在CoqStoq的100个定理上评估了6个开源权重大语言模型，600次尝试中仅产生21个经Coq内核验证的证明，总体成功率为3.5%。Gemma 4表现最好，但研究未进行统计显著性检验，不能据此建立模型的普遍排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型生成的文本形式上像数学证明，并不代表其逻辑正确。研究旨在通过Coq形式化证明检查器，评估不同开源权重模型生成可被内核接受的真实证明的能力。

Method: 选取源自真实Coq项目的CoqStoq基准中的100个定理，测试6个开源权重模型。每个模型对每个定理仅尝试一次，温度设为0，并在定理原始项目环境中由Coq检查证明。只有被Coq内核接受的证明才计为成功，同时统计证明覆盖情况、参考证明长度及生成成本。

Result: Gemma 4验证12个定理，Llama 3.3验证8个，DeepSeek Coder V2 Lite验证1个；Qwen 3.5、Mistral Small 3.1和GPT-OSS均未成功。21个模型—定理成功结果涉及15个不同定理，其中11个未被标准Coq策略基线解决。成功定理的人工参考证明均为短或中等长度，没有模型验证长参考证明。3个取得成功的模型，其每个验证证明的生成成本为741至36,193个输出词元、14.9至178.0秒和0.0167至0.2000个GPU小时。

Conclusion: 当前模型在Coq形式化证明任务上的可靠成功率较低，且成功可能集中于较短或中等复杂度的定理。结果仅支持描述性比较，不能证明参考证明长度具有因果影响，也不能据此断言任何模型具有普遍性能优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate text that resembles a mathematical proof, but resemblance does not establish correctness. A formal proof checker verifies whether each proof step follows established logical rules. Coq bases its rules on the Calculus of Inductive Constructions, a logical framework that defines which proof steps the system may accept.
  This pilot study evaluated six open-weight LLMs on the same 100 theorems from CoqStoq, a benchmark derived from real Coq projects. Each LLM received one attempt per theorem with the temperature set to 0, and Coq checked every proposed proof in the theorem's original project environment. We counted a proof as successful only if the Coq kernel accepted it.
  Gemma 4 verified 12 of 100 theorems, Llama 3.3 verified 8, and DeepSeek Coder V2 Lite verified 1. Qwen 3.5, Mistral Small 3.1, and GPT-OSS verified none. The 21 successful model-theorem results covered 15 distinct theorems, 11 of which were not solved by a baseline of standard Coq tactics. All verified theorems had short or medium human-written reference proofs; no model verified a theorem with a long reference proof. Because the proof-length analysis was exploratory, this pattern does not establish that proof length caused the difference.
  For the three models with at least one success, the total generation cost per verified proof ranged from 741 to 36,193 output tokens, 14.9 to 178.0 seconds, and 0.0167 to 0.2000 aggregate GPU hours. We could not calculate these ratios for models with no verified proofs. Across 600 attempts, the models produced 21 kernel-verified proofs, giving an overall success rate of 3.5%. The study reports descriptive differences among the models but does not statistically test whether one model outperforms another. Therefore, the results do not establish a universal ranking of the six models.

</details>


### [128] [A Bitopological Approach to Finite Reduction and Bounded Exact-Value Certificates for Fitting's Finite Heyting-valued Modal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05550)
*Litan Kumar Das,Kumar Sankar Ray,Prakash Chandra Mali*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限Heyting值模态逻辑，借助关系双拓扑表示将有限模型约化为有限状态，并保持公式的精确真值；同时为公式失败构造有深度和分支界的树状证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决有限Heyting值模态逻辑中的状态冗余与模型约化问题，在减少模型规模的同时，确保所有模态公式的真值及失败程度均被准确保留。

Method: 对有限模型和有限词汇，利用原子赋值生成的模态子代数定义状态—评价映射；通过关系双拓扑对偶表示构造观测商，并证明商关系是规范对偶关系的限制。此外，依据模态深度、Heyting代数高度及盒算子子公式数量，构造有限树状精确值证书。

Result: 观测商同构于双拓扑对偶中的有限像，且所有词汇内公式在商模型中保持完全相同的真值。该商是所有生成观测都能因子化通过的满射约化中的最小者。对任意公式和状态，还存在深度受模态深度约束、分支数受真值代数高度和盒子子公式数量控制的证书；失败公式具有保持精确失败值的有界约化反例。

Conclusion: 关系双拓扑方法为有限Heyting值模态逻辑提供了既保持精确语义又具有最小性的有限状态约化，并进一步给出了复杂度受控的失败证书与反例结构。

Abstract: Fitting's finite Heyting-valued modal logic interprets modal formulas
  over a finite Heyting algebra. We use a relational bitopological representation to obtain a finite-state reduction. For a finite model and a finite vocabulary, the modal subalgebra generated by the atomic valuations determines a state-evaluation map. We prove that the
  observational quotient is isomorphic to its finite image in the
  bitopological dual and that the quotient relation is the restriction
  of the canonical dual relation. Hence every formula over the
  vocabulary preserves its exact truth value, and the quotient is
  minimal among surjective reductions through which all generated
  observations factor. In addition, for any formula and state, we
  construct a finite tree-like exact-value certificate whose depth is
  bounded by modal depth and whose branching depends only on the height
  of the truth-value algebra and the number of boxed subformulas. Failed
  formulas therefore admit bounded reduced counterexamples preserving
  their precise failure values.

</details>


### [129] [Implicit Computation of Filtered Prime Implicants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05943)
*Edward Liem,Clemens Dubslaff*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出面向受约束输入空间的端到端符号化素蕴含项（PI）计算方法，通过决策图隐式表示满足侧约束的PI集合，避免先计算全部PI再进行后处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PI算法主要针对完整输入空间；在存在上下文或结构依赖时，通常需要先生成可能呈指数增长的全部PI，再过滤不满足约束的结果，导致严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 扩展基于决策图的Coudert-Madre方法，构建模块化工具链，将决策图构造、PI计算和约束过滤分离，同时在符号层面直接处理侧约束，从而隐式表示满足约束的PI集合。

Result: 该方法能够避免显式枚举和后处理全部PI，降低由PI数量指数增长带来的计算与存储压力，并为受约束场景下的PI计算提供统一的符号化实现框架。摘要未提供具体实验数据或基准比较结果。

Conclusion: 论文将PI计算从无约束输入空间推广到受侧约束的输入空间，核心贡献是端到端、决策图驱动的隐式计算框架，有望提升约束场景下PI算法的可扩展性。

Abstract: Prime implicants (PIs) are central in computer science, with applications in logic minimization, diagnosis, explainable formal methods and AI. Algorithms for the computation of PIs were first-and-foremost considered on the full input space, not considering the case where the input space might be constrained by context or structural dependencies. To filter out those PIs that do not fulfill the constraints, existing methods rely on an explicit post-processing step after computing all PIs, which leads to scalability issues due to the number of PIs being exponential. We propose end-to-end symbolic algorithms that implicitly represent the set of PIs under side constraints. For this, we extend the prominent Coudert-Madre method based on decision diagrams and implement a modular tool chain that separates decision-diagram construction, PI computation, and filtering.

</details>


### [130] [Extending RTLola with External Data Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06039)
*Bernd Finkbeiner,Jakob Hirschler,Frederik Scheerer,Sebastian Schirmer*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了流式监控框架RTLola，使其能够查询外部数据源，并通过统一接口集成静态数据库和动态API。实验表明，面向航空领域的定制化地理空间后端在性能上优于现有数据库系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式监控器擅长表达复杂时序属性，但处理大规模外部数据源的能力有限，而这类任务通常更适合由专门的数据管理系统完成。因此，研究旨在弥合流式监控与外部数据管理系统之间的能力差距。

Method: 在RTLola中引入外部数据查询能力，重点处理异步或延迟响应、返回数据的类型检查以及运行时错误管理。研究设计了统一接口，以集成静态数据库、动态数据端点和天气API等已有系统，并实现了基于k-d树的定制化地理空间后端。

Result: 系统能够将多种外部数据源无缝接入流式监控流程。基于航空领域监控规范的评估显示，定制化地理空间后端的性能优于当前先进的数据库系统。

Conclusion: 将外部数据查询能力纳入流式监控框架可以有效提升其处理复杂环境数据的能力。RTLola的统一接口和运行时机制支持了多样化数据源集成，而针对应用场景设计的专用后端能够获得显著性能优势。

Abstract: Stream-based monitoring enables the concise specification of complex temporal properties. However, existing stream-based monitors are limited when dealing with large external data sources, a task that is better handled by specialized data management systems. We address these limitations by extending stream-based monitors with the ability to query external data sources. We implement this approach in RTLola and investigate challenges such as handling delayed responses, type checking of returned data, and runtime error management. A unified interface enables the seamless integration of existing systems into our approach, such as static databases or dynamic endpoints, e.g. a weather API. Our evaluation using specifications from the aviation domain also shows that a custom geospatial backend based on k-d trees outperforms state-of-the-art database systems.

</details>


### [131] [Two Ways to See the Future: Combining Prediction and Future-Offset Accesses in RTLola](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06090)
*Jan Baumeister,Bernd Finkbeiner,Eduard Müller,Frederik Scheerer,Julia Tillman*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文将RTLola扩展为支持未来相关属性：通过预测算子基于历史观测推断任意时间戳的未来值，并通过离散未来偏移算子在信息可用后获取精确未来值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTLola面向异步实时系统，现有机制主要描述当前或过去行为，无法直接表达依赖未来行为的时序属性，因此需要兼顾即时预测与精确未来访问的扩展机制。

Method: 引入两类互补算子：预测算子根据过去观测外推未来流值，支持立即但可能不准确的预测；离散未来偏移算子延迟相关流表达式的求值，以便在所需信息到达后访问精确的未来值。论文同时对两种扩展进行形式化语义定义，并实现原型进行评估。

Result: 实验评估了扩展机制对运行时间和内存消耗的影响。摘要未给出具体测量数值，但表明两种机制均被纳入实现并进行了资源开销分析。

Conclusion: RTLola通过预测和未来偏移两种机制获得了表达未来依赖属性的能力：前者强调即时性和灵活性，后者强调结果精确性。该扩展在形式语义和实现层面均得到支持，为异步实时系统的未来行为监测提供了基础。

Abstract: RTLola is a stream-based specification language designed for asynchronous real-time systems. While many temporal specifications naturally refer to future behavior, RTLola currently offers no mechanism to express such future-dependent properties. In this paper, we extend RTLola with two complementary mechanisms to reason about the future. First, we introduce a prediction operator that extrapolates future stream values at arbitrary timestamps based on past observations. Second, we add a discrete future offset operator, which provides access to precise future values by delaying the evaluation of the dependent stream expressions. While the former enables immediate, but possibly imprecise predictions, the latter ensures exact values once the required information becomes available. We formalize both extensions in the RTLola semantics and evaluate their implementation on runtime and memory consumption.

</details>


### [132] [Quantalic lambda-calculus and additive disjunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06120)
*Renato Neves,Bruna Salgado*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文将带加性析取的量化线性λ演算扩展用于定量分析 case 语句，证明其等式系统的可靠性，并在量化子满足连续性条件时证明近似完备性；同时给出范畴逻辑、概率计算和量子计算等模型，并以随机游走的柯西序列为例展示其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统程序语义主要关注程序等价性，难以刻画 case 语句及概率行为等定量性质。本文旨在建立一种能够结合距离、概率或其他量化结构进行推理的统一演算框架。

Method: 在量化线性λ演算中引入加性析取，构造相应的等式推理系统；通过模型论与范畴逻辑中的 gluing 方法证明系统可靠性，并在量化子满足特定连续性条件时建立近似完备性。随后构造包括概率模型在内的多个语义模型，并利用基于 Banach 空间的概率模型分析随机游走序列。

Result: 扩展后的演算具有可靠的等式系统；在适当连续性假设下，该系统还具有近似完备性。研究给出了多类语义模型，并展示了如何结合演算规则与 Banach 空间概率模型，对随机游走的柯西序列进行定量推理。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个支持加性析取和定量推理的线性λ演算框架，连接了程序演算、泛函分析、概率计算及范畴逻辑。结果表明，程序语义可以从单纯研究程序等价性扩展到更灵活的定量分析。

Abstract: Motivated by the need to reason about case statements quantitatively, we extend quantalic linear lambda-calculus with additive disjunction. We show that the resulting equational system is sound. We also show that when certain continuity properties (of the underlying quantale) are adopted, it is additionally (approximately) complete.
  We present several models of the extended calculus, involving for example meta-theoretical properties in categorical logic (gluing), probabilistic, and quantum computation. As a concrete application, we illustrate how a probabilistic model, based on Banach spaces, can be synergistically used with the calculus' equational system to reason about Cauchy sequences of random walks. This highlights the emergent shift from "program semantics as the science of program equivalence" to flexible, quantitative perspectives, involving functional analysis and beyond.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Post-Hoc Trajectory-Risk Certification for Modular LLM-Based Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05199)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究多阶段自主安全代理中的轨迹级置信覆盖问题，指出独立阶段的保序校准不能自动组合，并提出基于生成树的有效依赖校正方法。实验表明，充分审计样本下直接审计可比Bonferroni方法收紧13.7%，但样本不足时反而更差；跨数据集部署会使校准置信度失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单阶段的有限样本覆盖保证无法直接转化为完整处理链的轨迹级保证。Bonferroni方法虽分布无关但在阶段错误相关时过于保守，而简单的多阶段相关性扩展可能产生错误的下界。论文还关注如何区分真实阶段依赖与审计样本不足造成的误判，以及标签选择策略导致的虚假高相关。

Method: 论文证明三阶段及以上的自然两两相关扩展不能提供所需的上界，并构造基于生成树的有效替代方案。研究分别分析阶段依赖关系和审计样本是否足以认证该依赖，给出匹配的上界与信息论下界样本复杂度。实验在两个数据集和六个开放大型语言模型上进行，采用去除粗到细标签选择伪影、直接轨迹失败审计、模块化阶段证书、两两重叠界，以及同模型、跨模型和置换配对测试。

Result: 去除标签选择伪影后，测得相关性由接近1降至0至0.78。审计样本充足时，直接轨迹失败审计相比Bonferroni紧13.7%；样本不足时性能更差。仅使用阶段证书和两两重叠界的模块化证书平均提升0.6%。残余依赖主要来自样本难度共享，而非模型表示共享。12种配置下，alpha=0.10时平均轨迹覆盖率为92.7%±2.4%。跨数据集部署时，单步失覆盖率达到100%，尽管准确率仍为78%。

Conclusion: 多阶段置信保证需要显式处理轨迹级联合事件、依赖结构和审计样本复杂度。生成树方法为相关阶段提供了有效且可组合的保证，但其收益依赖充足的联合审计数据。实验同时表明，标签层级造成的伪相关必须被剔除，且分布迁移可能先破坏置信校准而非立即显著降低准确率。

Abstract: Autonomous security agents operate as staged pipelines, such as classifying network traffic and then attributing attacks to a specific technique. Split conformal prediction gives each stage finite-sample coverage, but deployment requires a trajectory-level guarantee across the full chain. These guarantees do not compose automatically when stages are independently trained and calibrated. Bonferroni allocation is distribution-free but conservative under correlated errors. We show that a natural pairwise-correlation extension to three or more stages is invalid because it gives a lower rather than an upper bound, and derive a valid spanning-tree alternative. We distinguish whether stages are dependent from whether an audit sample is large enough to certify that dependence, and give matching upper and information-theoretic lower sample-complexity bounds. We also show that coarse-to-fine label selection can create near-perfect measured correlation without learned dependence.
  On a two-stage intrusion-detection pipeline across 6 open LLMs and 2 datasets, removing this artifact reduces measured correlation from near 1 to 0-0.78. A direct audit of trajectory failure becomes 13.7% tighter than Bonferroni once the audit reaches the required sample size, but is worse when undersized. A modular certificate using per-stage certificates and a pairwise overlap bound yields a positive average gain of 0.6%, quantifying the cost of lacking joint access. Same-model, cross-model, and permuted-pairing tests show that residual dependence reflects shared sample difficulty, not shared model representations. Average trajectory coverage across 12 configurations is 92.7% +/- 2.4% at alpha = 0.10. Under cross-dataset deployment, single-step miscoverage reaches 100% even when accuracy remains 78%, showing that distribution shift destroys calibrated confidence before raw accuracy.

</details>


### [134] [ASTELD: A Six-Axis Classification Framework for Autonomous AI Agents - Design, Evaluation, and an OpenClaw Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05201)
*Siyuan Li,Peng Shu,Churan Yu,Peilong Wang,Ruidong Zhang,Bowen Guo,Xinliang Li,Ruiyu Yan,Arif Hassan Zidan,Yi Pan,Wei Ruan,Lifeng Chen,Junhao Chen,Zhaojun Ding,Yiwei Li,Zhengliang Liu,Haixing Dai,Lin Zhao,Yu Bao,Xiang Li,Wei Zhang,Tianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASTELD六轴分类框架，用于系统比较自主AI代理平台在架构、安全、工具集成、执行、自主性与部署拓扑方面的设计差异。对8个平台及50多个OpenClaw衍生系统的分析表明，该框架具有较好的区分性和解释力，并揭示了平台生态中的共性模式与未被占据的设计区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主AI代理平台在架构、安全策略、工具调用、执行机制、人机控制和部署方式上差异显著，但缺乏统一、可操作且可复现的比较标准。这限制了平台选择、生态分析、风险评估和后续实证研究。

Method: 研究综合既有代理分类法与可观测的平台属性，构建ASTELD六轴框架：Architecture pattern、Security posture、Tool integration model、Execution paradigm、Level of autonomy and human control、Deployment topology，并制定明确的类别分配规则。研究将8个代表性框架映射到ASTELD中，以OpenClaw开展深入案例研究，同时对50多个衍生系统进行分类，并结合多项机构评估、采用情况和治理分析检验框架的解释能力。

Result: ASTELD在主导配置下能够区分全部8个平台，并揭示三个跨平台模式：安全性与可访问性之间存在权衡关系，执行范式与架构模式高度耦合，以及能力趋同但架构仍保持差异。OpenClaw衍生生态的创新主要集中在安全、执行和部署三个轴上。案例研究还形成了六类漏洞分类，并将平台坐标与实际风险、采用和治理现象联系起来。研究发现，当前评估系统中不存在同时具备本地优先部署和企业级安全性的方案。

Conclusion: ASTELD为自主AI代理平台提供了一种可复现、可比较的操作性分析方法，可用于识别设计空白、指导框架选型并组织未来实证研究。其主要价值在于将平台设计属性、生态分化和治理风险纳入同一分析框架；但结论仍依赖于所选平台、可观测属性及类别判定规则，后续需要通过更大规模样本和实证验证进一步检验其稳定性。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agent platforms differ substantially in architecture, security, tool integration, execution, autonomy, and deployment, yet the field lacks a common classification scheme for comparing these design choices. We propose ASTELD, an operational six-axis classification framework for autonomous AI agents: Architecture pattern, Security posture, Tool integration model, Execution paradigm, Level of autonomy and human control, and Deployment topology. ASTELD is constructed by synthesizing prior agent taxonomies with observable platform properties and explicit category-assignment rules. We evaluate its discriminative and explanatory utility by mapping eight representative frameworks and by using OpenClaw as an in-depth case study. The resulting profiles separate all eight platforms under their dominant configurations and reveal three cross-platform patterns: a security-accessibility diagonal, strong execution-architecture coupling, and capability convergence with persistent architectural differentiation. We further classify 50+ OpenClaw derivatives and find that innovation concentrates on the Security, Execution, and Deployment axes, indicating that ASTELD can explain where ecosystem fragmentation occurs. The OpenClaw case study also supplies a six-category vulnerability taxonomy, evidence from five institutional assessments, and adoption and governance analyses that connect platform coordinates to observed risks. These results position ASTELD as a reproducible method for comparing agent platforms, identifying unoccupied design regions, guiding framework selection, and organizing future empirical research. The analysis also exposes a consequential empty region: none of the evaluated systems combines local-first deployment with enterprise-grade security.

</details>


### [135] [A Survey of Adversarial Efficiency Degradation for Vision Transformer by Exploiting Input-adaptive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05217)
*Anadi Goyal,Nandish Chattopadhyay,Anupam Chattopadhyay,Chandan Karfa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述针对视觉Transformer输入自适应推理机制的对抗性效率退化攻击，比较SlowFormer与DeSparsify在A-ViT、ATS和AdaViT上的表现，并以GFLOPs、精度损失和攻击成功率统一评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Token剪枝和提前停止可降低视觉Transformer的计算量与能耗，但其输入依赖性也暴露出新的攻击面。攻击者可能在不显著降低模型精度的情况下，诱导模型保留更多Token或延迟停止，从而削弱计算节省，尤其威胁移动端和嵌入式等低功耗部署环境。

Method: 围绕三个问题开展系统分析：输入自适应优化如何产生效率退化攻击面；SlowFormer通用对抗补丁和DeSparsify逐图像扰动如何实施，以及不同优化机制的脆弱性；现有防御能否恢复受攻击时的效率。实验对象包括A-ViT、ATS和AdaViT，并统一采用GFLOPs、精度损失及Attack Success指标，其中Attack Success衡量攻击夺回模型原有计算节省的程度。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数值或明确排名，但其分析框架用于横向比较两类攻击在三种Token剪枝框架上的计算开销、精度影响、攻击成功率和防御效果，并识别最易受影响的输入自适应机制。

Conclusion: 输入自适应推理虽然能够有效降低视觉Transformer的计算成本，但也可能被对抗扰动利用，造成隐蔽的计算效率退化。防御设计需要同时关注攻击鲁棒性、精度保持和额外开销，尤其应满足低功耗设备对轻量化的要求。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) increasingly rely on input-adaptive inference, such as token pruning and early halting, to meet energy and latency budgets. This survey examines a recent class of adversarial efficiency degradation attacks that target these mechanisms to increase computation without necessarily degrading accuracy. We unify and compare two representative attacks, SlowFormer (a universal adversarial patch) and DeSparsify (per-image perturbations), across three popular token-pruning frameworks: A-ViT, ATS, and AdaViT. We standardize reporting using GFLOPs, accuracy loss, and an Attack Success (AS) metric that measures how much of the model's compute savings the attack takes away. Understanding these attacks is crucial for designing countermeasures that not only mitigate risk but also remain lightweight, since deployment often occurs in low-power settings such as mobile or embedded devices. To organize our analysis, we focus on three questions: how input-adaptive optimizations (e.g., token pruning and early halting) create attack surfaces for efficiency degradation; how such attacks operate in practice and which optimizations are most vulnerable; and which defenses exist today and whether they meaningfully restore efficiency under attack.

</details>


### [136] [CyberBridge: Bridging the Gap Between Cybersecurity Education and Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05231)
*Arthur Nijdam,Paul Stankovski Wagner,Sara Ramezanian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberBridge是一个可解释的框架，利用句子级BERT将网络安全职位描述拆解为知识、技能和任务（KST）要素，并匹配到相似的职业角色画像，以支持职业推荐、劳动力市场分析和课程规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全行业变化迅速，教育项目难以及时对齐实际岗位所需能力。现有课程与就业需求之间存在信息和匹配不足的问题，因此需要一种能够连接职位要求、职业角色与课程能力的实用工具。

Method: 首先将职位描述分解为知识、技能和任务陈述；随后使用Sentence-BERT生成语义嵌入，并将这些要素与专业劳动力角色画像进行相似度匹配。系统同时保留具体能力要素与匹配结果之间的关联，从而提供可追溯、可解释的人类可读依据。

Result: CyberBridge支持三类应用：根据学生已完成的课程推荐匹配职位和职业角色；分析当前网络安全岗位及角色需求；评估不同课程项目对热门网络安全职业的准备程度。摘要表明，该方法能够实现基于能力要素的岗位—角色映射，并增强推荐结果的透明度。

Conclusion: CyberBridge为网络安全教育提供了连接就业市场与课程设计的可解释工具，可用于职业指导、需求分析和基于证据的课程开发。不过，摘要未报告具体数据集、评估指标或实验结果，因此其实际匹配精度和泛化能力仍需通过完整实验验证。

Abstract: This full research paper presents CyberBridge, a framework that automatically maps cybersecurity job descrip- tions to professional role profiles. As the cybersecurity landscape evolves rapidly, it is difficult for academic programs to align curricula with the competencies expected in practice.
  CyberBridge addresses this gap by decomposing a given vacancy description into its constituent Knowledge, Skill, and Task (KST) statements, embedding them using a sentence- BERT model, and matching them to the most semantically similar workforce profiles. A key contribution of our approach is its interpretability. Rather than functioning as a black box, CyberBridge enables users to trace recommendations back to the specific competencies driving each match, providing a human- readable justification for the resulting mappings.
  CyberBridge supports three primary use cases; (1) job recom- mendation, providing students with vacancies and closely match- ing professional roles based on their completed curriculum, (2) market analysis, enabling educators and curriculum developers to analyze which roles are currently in demand, and (3) curriculum planning, assessing which course program best prepares students for in-demand cybersecurity roles.
  As such, CyberBridge can be used by educational institutions as a practical tool for career guidance and evidence-based curriculum development in cybersecurity education.

</details>


### [137] [The Hidden Life of Public Safety Communications Signals: A Comparative Security Analysis of TETRA, TETRAPOL, and P25](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05247)
*Larry Hernandez,Sergey Bratus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示，P25、TETRA和TETRAPOL等集群陆地移动无线电系统虽然加密通信内容，但信令平面大多明文传输，攻击者可通过被动监听推断网络拓扑、地理位置、终端活动、组织结构及密钥管理信息，甚至在TETRAPOL中结合协议缺陷提取未加密语音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注通信内容的密钥恢复和明文泄露，而忽视了信令元数据本身的敏感性。论文旨在评估内容加密完善时，公开信令对通信机密性、身份隐匿和运营安全造成的实际影响。

Method: 研究者使用仅接收的商用软件定义无线电设备，被动采集三类主流集群LMR标准的信令数据，分析其中的时间、关联、身份和跨小区信息，并据此推断网络拓扑、地理范围、终端移动、群组关系、组织结构、密钥域及密钥周期；同时针对TETRAPOL分析紧急呼叫处理中的标准级保密缺陷。

Result: 实验表明，被动观察者能够恢复超出直接接收范围的全国性网络信息，包括小区间移动、群组活动和组织关联，并削弱或破坏基于时间与关联机制的身份混淆。在TETRAPOL中，信令推断与紧急呼叫保密缺陷结合后，可实现未加密语音提取。

Conclusion: LMR系统的保密性不能仅依赖内容加密，还必须保护信令平面及通信元数据。标准和部署方案应加强小区间、基站及终端身份保护，改进紧急呼叫处理，并系统性降低信令中的可关联信息与跨区域推断风险。

Abstract: Public-safety agencies and critical infrastructure operators rely on trunked land-mobile radio (LMR) systems, based on P25, TETRA, and TETRAPOL. These systems are expected to protect not just the content of a communication but the fact of it. Yet LMR standards leave a stark gap between confidentiality of \emph{content} and of \emph{communication}: underneath an encrypted traffic plane, their signaling plane is almost entirely in the clear. We probe the depth and impact of adversarial inference from this exposed signaling.
  Prior security analyses of these systems have concentrated on the content plane---recovering encryption keys or capturing accidental cleartext. We show that comparably sensitive information can be \emph{inferred from passively observed signaling even if the content encryption were perfect}.
  In particular, we show that across the trunked LMR standards, a passive, receive-only software-defined radio (SDR) observer can recover operationally sensitive network topology and geography details, unit presence, mobility across cells and groups, organizational structure, as well as operational security details such as special key domains and key-epoch rotation. This signaling-plane inference reaches far beyond the observer's direct area of reception, turning \emph{local} sniffing into \emph{nationwide} network mapping capabilities that degrade or defeat LMR standards' identity obfuscation through timing and association. In the case of TETRAPOL, we demonstrate how inference and tracking of such signaling metadata and a standards-level confidentiality failure in emergency call handling enable unencrypted voice extraction.
  Finally, we discuss potential countermeasures and mitigations, including specific recommendations for protecting inter-cell, base station and subscriber identities.

</details>


### [138] [eMicro: Real-Time Multi-Hop Access Control for Microservices with eBPF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05300)
*Rizky Ramadhana Putra,Osama Bajaber,Saimon Amanuel Tsegai,Teryl Taylor,Frederico Araujo,Yuede Ji,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: eMicro是一种面向微服务的路径感知访问控制系统，通过记录服务调用序列、使用DFA编码安全策略，并借助eBPF进行内核级请求追踪，以防御多跳攻击。实验表明其具备较高的可扩展性和较低的运行时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的服务间访问控制仅验证单跳请求，无法识别由多个合法跳转组合形成的违规调用路径，因而难以防止未授权访问和数据泄露。大规模微服务环境还要求防护机制具备高性能、透明部署和良好扩展性。

Method: eMicro将基于历史的访问控制扩展为服务调用序列控制；使用确定性有限自动机（DFA）对安全策略进行紧凑编码，实现常数时间策略查询和高效标签传播；利用eBPF在内核中透明追踪请求并执行策略，无需修改应用代码。

Result: 在DeathStarBench以及来自Uber、阿里巴巴和字节跳动的生产云追踪数据上，系统覆盖约1200万个请求工作流和数千个服务。策略检查延迟约为1微秒，可存储5000万条策略并仅占用100 MB，传播开销降低90%，整体运行时影响可忽略。

Conclusion: eMicro能够有效识别并阻止多跳攻击，同时保持较低延迟、存储开销和部署成本，适合在大规模微服务环境中实现高效、可扩展的路径级访问控制。

Abstract: Modern cloud applications often comprise thousands of microservices whose interactions form complex request paths. Traditional inter-service access control restricts individual service-to-service requests, but fails to prevent multi-hop attacks, where each hop appears legitimate yet the overall path violates security intent. This gap leaves systems exposed to unauthorized access and data exfiltration. In this paper, we present eMicro, a path-aware defense system for microservices that prevents such attacks while remaining efficient and deployable. eMicro enforces real-time multi-hop access control through three key techniques: (1) history-based access control extended to capture service invocation sequences; (2) security policies encoded as efficient deterministic finite automaton (DFA), supporting constant-time lookups and compact label propagation; (3) eBPF-based in-kernel request tracing for transparent, low-overhead enforcement without code changes. Evaluations on DeathStarBench and production cloud traces from Uber, Alibaba, and ByteDance, covering 12 million request workflows and thousands of services, demonstrate the scalability of eMicro. eMicro performs policy checks in 1 microsecond, stores 50 million policies in only 100 MB, and reduces propagation overhead by 90% with negligible runtime impact. These results show that eMicro delivers scalable and efficient protection against multi-hop attacks, making it practical for deployment in large-scale microservice environments.

</details>


### [139] [The Trust-Free Aggregation Layer of the Unicity Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05316)
*Risto Laanoja,Mike Gault,Dirk Draheim,Ahto Buldas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Unicity，一种支持链下点对点代币交易的区块链基础设施，通过聚合层记录代币已花费状态，并利用密码学证明防止双重支付，同时降低主链复杂度与存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链下代币交易需要在减少区块链负担的同时，确保代币状态不可并行或重复使用。现有方案通常面临存储开销、验证成本或信任假设较高的问题，因此本文旨在构建一种无需额外信任设置、具备高吞吐和低验证延迟的状态聚合机制。

Method: 系统采用两层架构：聚合层维护不可追加修改的键值存储，共识层通过传统区块链周期性认证其状态。聚合层使用基数稀疏默克尔树生成每轮状态摘要，并通过特殊认证路径证明新摘要仅由追加键值对得到，从而保证存储的追加一致性。证明验证被实现为基于 Plonky3 STARK 工具包的代数中间表示电路。

Result: 实现无需可信设置，在单台消费级 CPU 上达到每秒约 10,000 次插入，并实现毫秒级证明验证时间。该方案能够为用户提供紧凑的无双重支付密码学证明，同时减少共识层需要处理的状态信息。

Conclusion: Unicity 通过 RSMT、追加一致性证明和 STARK 验证电路，将链下交易效率与链上可验证性结合起来。实验结果表明，该聚合层在吞吐量、验证延迟和信任模型方面具有较好的实用性，但摘要未进一步说明证明大小、网络开销及在大规模并发场景下的扩展性。

Abstract: Unicity is a novel blockchain infrastructure for enabling users to execute off-chain peer-to-peer token transactions while preventing parallel states of tokens (double-spending) with minimal blockchain complexity and storage. A key component of the infrastructure is the Aggregation Layer responsible for storing information about the spent states of tokens and providing compact cryptographic proofs of no double-spending for the users without making any compromises in trust. Aggregation Layer is a layer 2 style service that holds an append-only key-value repository that periodically certifies its state using a traditional blockchain that we call the Consensus Layer. Every time while certifying a changed state the Aggregation Layer provides a cryptographic proof to the Consensus Layer about the append-only consistency of the key-value store. We use the Radix Sparse Merkle Trees (RSMTs) to create the cryptographic digest r_i of the store in every round i and authentication paths of special type in order to prove that the next digest r_{i+1} was obtained while only adding some key-value pairs (k,v) to the repository. The proof verification code is implemented as an Algebraic Intermediate Representation (AIR) circuit on top of the Plonky3 STARK toolkit. Our implementation uses no trusted setup, achieves throughput of 10,000 insertions per second and and millisecond range verification time on a single consumer-class CPU.

</details>


### [140] [S12X Patch Diffing with QBinDiff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05350)
*Ben Gardiner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对商用车辆制动ECU的固件更新进行逆向工程分析，提取固件并对S12X架构固件镜像开展差分二进制分析，发现召回更新同时修复了未公开的协议处理漏洞，但补丁功能仍存在严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示安全召回固件更新的实际修改内容，确认其是否包含安全漏洞修复，并评估修复实现的可靠性。

Method: 分析固件更新器可执行文件以提取固件，随后对更新前后的S12X架构固件镜像进行差分二进制分析，重点检查遗留协议处理逻辑及其修改。

Result: 研究识别出召回版本针对遗留协议处理中的未公开漏洞进行了修改，表明该安全召回措施同时具有安全补丁性质；但分析还发现补丁后的功能存在关键缺陷。

Conclusion: 车辆安全召回与嵌入式安全修复可能高度相关，不能仅依据功能描述评估更新效果。该案例表明，漏洞修复补丁仍需经过系统性的安全验证与缺陷审查。

Abstract: This paper presents a reverse engineering analysis of a firmware update for a commercial vehicle Brake ECU. We analyze the updater executable to extract firmware and perform differential binary analysis of the S12X architecture firmware images. Our research identifies specific changes in the recall that address undocumented vulnerabilities in legacy protocol processing. We demonstrate that the patched functionality contained critical flaws. The findings confirm the safety recall remediation was also a security patch.

</details>


### [141] [Robust Context-Aware Detection of Malicious Instructions in Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05430)
*Buzhao Liu,Xinhang Ma,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向间接提示注入的恶意句子检测方法，结合上下文与用户查询进行分类，并通过两类对抗训练提升对自适应规避攻击的鲁棒性，在多个基准上优于现有防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型被广泛用于自主智能体，但容易受到嵌入文本中的恶意指令影响。现有检测器缺乏查询相关的句子级检测能力，且通常未针对智能体执行场景中的自适应规避攻击进行加固。

Method: 首先构建同时利用文本上下文和用户查询的恶意句子分类器。随后提出两种对抗训练方法：一是在嵌入空间中利用基于投影梯度的优化近似规避样本；二是在训练循环中使用大语言模型改写文本，以模拟现实可实施的规避攻击。两种方法均通过参数化实现检测效用与攻击鲁棒性之间的平滑权衡。

Result: 在间接提示注入基准上的实验表明，该方法在静态攻击下优于现有防御基线。在自适应攻击下，两种对抗训练方法通常能够获得更高的任务效用和更低的攻击成功率，部分情况下同时改善二者。实验还发现，最优对抗训练参数与具体应用领域密切相关。

Conclusion: 查询相关、上下文感知的句子级检测结合针对性对抗训练，能够有效提升间接提示注入防御能力。实际部署时应依据应用领域调节对抗训练强度和检测器参数。

Abstract: The remarkable instruction-following ability of modern LLMs has enabled their practical use as the minds of agents that can autonomously complete increasingly complex tasks. Therein, however, also lies their vulnerability to attacks which embed malicious instructions in text, common variants of which are known as indirect prompt injection (IPI). A fundamental task in addressing this vulnerability is successful segmentation of a given text into benign and malicious sentences (if any). While a number of approaches for this task have been proposed, no detector combines query-relative detection at the segment level, and none are hardened against adaptive evasion attacks realizable in agentic executions. We address the former limitation by developing an approach for malicious sentence classification that is both context- and query-aware. Next, to harden the resulting classifier against evasion, we present two adversarial training methods. The first is directly adapted feature-space adversarial training (AT) in which evasions are approximated using projected-gradient-based optimization in the embedding space. The second simulates realizable evasion attacks in the AT loop through LLM-based paraphrasing. Crucially, we parametrize both AT variants to facilitate a smooth tradeoff between utility and attack robustness. In extensive experiments using indirect prompt injection benchmarks we show that the proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art IPI defense baselines under static attacks, while in the case of adaptive attacks, our AT variants provide significantly higher utility, lower attack success rate, and often both. Finally, we show that the best AT parameters can depend intimately on the particular application domain. Consequently, domain-dependent tuning of malicious text detectors is likely necessary in practice. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tavia-liu/CAD.

</details>


### [142] [Game Hopping in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06261)
*Stefan Dziembowski,Grzegorz Fabiański,Daniele Micciancio,Rafał Stefański*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HOPSCOTCH 是一个基于 Lean 4 的计算安全、基于博弈的密码学证明框架，通过浅嵌入和显式游戏跳转对象，实现密码安全证明的形式化、自动化与可检查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码学安全证明涉及状态、概率、归约和复杂数学理论，难以在证明助手中统一形式化。HOPSCOTCH 旨在提供兼容 Lean 生态的通用框架，并对游戏跳转证明给出计算可靠性保证。

Method: 框架将安全性定义为有状态概率预言机之间的不可区分性，并把预言机和归约表示为普通 Lean 定义。游戏跳转被编码为具有标准证明步骤构造子的形式对象；框架通过状态抽象证明预言机的观测等价性，支持状态添加或遗忘、即时采样与惰性采样转换，并构造针对安全假设的归约及优势界。

Result: 形式化并验证了 encrypt-then-MAC 的 IND-CCA 安全性、基于 DDH 的 ElGamal 安全性、一次性保密性推出公钥 IND-CPA 安全性，以及 GGM 伪随机函数构造的安全性。作者指出，GGM 的非恒定深度形式化证明据其所知尚属首次。

Conclusion: HOPSCOTCH 将标准游戏跳转方法、Lean 的通用数学库和计算可靠性定理结合起来，为密码学安全证明提供了可复用、可审查且具有具体优势界的机械化基础。

Abstract: We present HOPSCOTCH, a Lean 4 framework for mechanizing computationally sound, game-based cryptographic proofs. Security definitions are expressed as indistinguishability between stateful probabilistic oracles, and proofs follow the standard game-hopping paradigm. HOPSCOTCH uses a shallow embedding: oracles and reductions are ordinary Lean definitions, enabling direct integration with the full Lean ecosystem, including general mathematical theories from Mathlib, such as finite-group theory. A game-hopping proof in HOPSCOTCH is represented as an explicit formal object whose constructors correspond to the standard steps of a game-hopping argument, making proofs easier to construct, automate, and inspect. We prove a general computational soundness theorem that interprets these proof objects by constructing reductions against the assumptions they use and deriving a concrete bound on the advantage of any distinguisher. Observational equivalence between oracles is established using a state-abstraction methodology: a simple yet powerful approach that supports transformations such as adding or forgetting state and replacing eager sampling with lazy sampling. We illustrate the framework with formalized proofs of the IND-CCA security of encrypt-then-MAC, the security of ElGamal encryption from DDH, the implication from one-time secrecy to public-key IND-CPA security, and the GGM pseudorandom-function construction. To the best of our knowledge, the last is the first mechanized proof of GGM for non-constant depth.

</details>


### [143] [Quantifying Bitcoin Network Resilience Through Critical Scenario Discovery: A Dual-Layer Framework for Discovering Contentious Fork Conditions in Decentralized Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05461)
*Peter Foytik,Sachin Shetty,Ross Gore,Eranga Bandara*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文利用场景发现与PRIM方法，通过Warnet运行1,330个比特币网络仿真场景，识别争议性软分叉导致干净解决或持续分裂的关键参数区间。结果表明，分区间的经济支持权重比算力多数更能决定分叉结局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 争议性软分叉的结果取决于协议规则之外的节点异质性、经济利益、矿池立场与网络条件，难以通过纯解析方法预测。因此，研究旨在通过受控的大规模实验识别导致链分裂的参数阈值，并形成可由公开数据监测的判断依据。

Method: 研究采用Scenario Discovery集成仿真框架和Patient Rule Induction Method（PRIM），使用Warnet部署真实bitcoind节点，覆盖经济权重、矿池承诺、矿池意识形态、算力及难度调整机制等变量，构造并运行1,330个有效场景，再据结果划分干净解决与争议性分裂的参数区域。

Result: 经济权重分布是主要决定因素，而非单纯的算力多数。在当前操作性难度调整周期和中等价格分化条件下，争议区域受0.45—0.50的经济支持下限与0.78—0.82的覆盖上限约束，经济自我维持点约为0.74。承诺算力达到0.214时出现矿池承诺翻转点：将最大矿池转向升级链反而可能逆转结果。分叉结局分为算力层与经济采用层，二者由不同参数控制；在设定的经济权重下，个体用户节点对结果没有可检测影响。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，评估争议性软分叉不能仅观察算力比例，还应重点监测分区经济支持、矿池承诺及经济采用情况。提出的阈值为识别潜在链分裂风险提供了操作化框架，但其适用性受价格分化、难度调整机制和经济权重设定等模型条件限制。

Abstract: Bitcoin's consensus depends not only on protocol rules but on the emergent behavior of a heterogeneous network of nodes with divergent economic stakes. Whether a contentious soft fork resolves cleanly or fractures into a persistent chain split is difficult to predict analytically but can be explored through controlled experimentation. This paper applies Scenario Discovery, an ensemble-simulation methodology using the Patient Rule Induction Method (PRIM), to identify the configuration regions that produce contentious fork outcomes. Using Warnet to run real bitcoind nodes across 1,330 valid scenarios spanning economic weight, mining-pool commitment, pool ideology, hashrate, and difficulty-retarget regime, we identify the parameter thresholds separating clean resolution from contested split. We show that economic weight distribution across a partition, not hashrate majority, is the primary determinant of resolution under Bitcoin's operational retarget interval and moderate price divergence. Three findings follow: an economic-support floor (0.45-0.50) and override ceiling (0.78-0.82) bound the contested space, with an Economic Self-Sustaining Point (0.74) between them; a pool-commitment "flip-point" (0.214 of committed hashrate) at which committing the largest pool to the upgrading chain paradoxically reverses the outcome; and outcomes resolve on two independent layers, hashrate and economic adoption, governed by different parameters. Individual user nodes show no detectable influence on fork outcomes under the modeled economic weightings. We close with three monitoring questions, answerable from public data, that translate these thresholds into operational guidance.

</details>


### [144] [Exploring Privacy Leakage and Data Disclosure Violations in the MacOS Application Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05474)
*Jyotirmay Chauhan,Kostas Solomos,Mir Masood Ali,Jason Polakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了macOS生态中应用数据访问、收集、共享与隐私披露机制，提出NutriScan框架分析应用实际数据行为与声明之间的不一致。对1000款macOS应用的动态分析显示，大量应用存在未披露的数据访问和向广告及托管实体外传数据的现象，表明桌面应用已成为在线追踪生态的重要组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私研究主要关注Web、移动端和物联网环境，对桌面应用尤其是macOS生态关注不足。macOS中多种数据访问与披露机制彼此割裂、数据抽象不一致，既增加了开发者合规难度，也可能助长未披露的数据收集和隐私侵入行为，因此需要进行系统性评估。

Method: 研究采用以应用开发为中心的分析视角，系统梳理macOS中调节和披露应用数据访问的多种机制及其交互关系。在此基础上构建NutriScan，将静态分析与动态分析结合，用于整合应用的数据访问行为、数据外传目的地及隐私披露信息，并对1000款macOS应用进行动态分析。

Result: 85%的应用访问用户数据API却未进行相应披露；49.7%的应用将数据外传至广告实体或托管服务提供商，其中12.5%的应用既进行外传又缺少对应披露。研究还发现，桌面应用被在线追踪者用于丰富用户画像和设备指纹，且macOS生态中的隐私机制存在分散、抽象不一致和相互脱节等问题。

Conclusion: macOS桌面应用构成了在线追踪和隐私风险的重要但被低估的来源。现有机制难以形成一致、完整的数据实践披露体系。论文建议简化开发者的数据披露流程，并改进苹果的应用审核机制，以提升披露准确性、监管有效性和用户隐私保护水平。

Abstract: The systematic and excessive data collection practices of tech companies have rendered online privacy both a necessity and a sought-after commodity. However, while the privacy risks of the web, mobile, and IoT ecosystems have been extensively examined, desktop environments have been largely overlooked. As desktop apps continue to be widely used, they remain a critical yet understudied dimension of user privacy. In this paper, we address this gap by presenting the first, to our knowledge, comprehensive study of the mechanisms designed to regulate and disclose data collection and sharing practices in the macOS ecosystem. We adopt an app-development-centric view, and shed light on the interactions between the various macOS mechanisms that mediate apps' data access. Driven by our findings, we develop NutriScan, an analysis framework that incorporates both static and dynamic analysis techniques to create a consolidated view of macOS apps' data practices and disclosures. We use our system to dynamically analyze 1K macOS apps, and find that 85% of them access user-data APIs without disclosing it. 49.7% also exfiltrate data to advertising entities and hosting providers, 12.5% of which do so without a corresponding disclosure. We find that desktop apps are being leveraged by online trackers to enrich user profiles and device fingerprints, thus shedding new light on the true scope of the online tracking ecosystem. Our analysis reveals how the macOS app ecosystem is comprised of disjoint mechanisms with divergent data abstractions, thus increasing complexity for developers while also facilitating undisclosed privacy-invasive practices. Accordingly, we propose a series of mitigations that aim to both streamline the data disclosure process for developers and improve Apple's app vetting process.

</details>


### [145] [PromptShield Home: Ambient Multimodal Prompt Injection Defense for Smart-Home Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05495)
*He Zhang,Feilong Li,Dingning Long,Yilin Cui,Peijun Zhang,Yuewen Zhang,Qianyao Xu,Xinyi Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 PromptShield-Home 基准，评估智能家居中如何区分真实用户指令与环境或外部注入内容。结果显示，传统检测器倾向于误执行，MLLM 则严重过度拒绝；两者正确样本几乎互补，理想路由器的上限准确率可达94.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能家居代理可直接理解视频和音频，因此可能把电视语音、屏幕文字、旁人对话或健康监测误报当作用户指令。现有单一安全机制难以同时避免不安全执行和对真实指令的过度拦截，且类别分布不均使总体准确率具有误导性。

Method: 构建 PromptShield-Home 试点基准，覆盖称呼对象歧义、屏幕与音频注入、健康监测误触发、多人居住环境及合法指令等场景。比较三种抽象层：传统检测器 L0；单一多模态大语言模型 L1，包括视觉、视觉加自动语音识别和音视频输入；多代理协调 L2，包括投票、角色专家和跨模型仲裁。除总体准确率外，分别报告不安全执行率和安全完成率，并计算理想选择不同层级的预言式上限。

Result: 由于数据中多数样本应采取不行动，恒定阻断策略也能取得82%的准确率。传统检测器倾向于对所有内容采取行动；所有 MLLM 配置则明显过度拒绝，几乎不完成真实指令，并在每个测试案例中都漏检一次真实跌倒事件。两类方法的正确样本集合互不重叠：最佳单层准确率为76.5%，理想情况下逐样本选择正确层级可达94.1%。该94.1%是上限估计，而非已实现系统。

Conclusion: 智能家居安全不宜简单地用 MLLM 替代传统检测器。由于两类方法具有互补优势，未来应研究学习式路由、传感器融合和跨模型协同，以在降低不安全执行的同时提高真实指令的安全完成率。

Abstract: Smart-home assistants increasingly use multimodal large language models (MLLMs) that perceive video and audio directly. This raises a safety question specific to the home: can the agent tell a genuine user command from ambient or externally-sourced content, television speech, on-screen text, or an overheard conversation, that merely looks like a command? We introduce PromptShield-Home, a pilot benchmark of realistic smart-home scenarios spanning addressee ambiguity, screen/audio injection, health-monitor false triggers, mixed occupancy, and a legitimate-command floor, and use it to compare three abstraction layers: traditional detectors (L0), a single MLLM agent (L1; vision, vision+ASR, and audio-visual), and multi-agent mediation (L2; voting, role specialists, cross-model arbitration). Because the label distribution is skewed toward inaction, aggregate accuracy is misleading, a constant always-block predictor scores 82%, so we report unsafe-execution and safe-completion rates separately. The two paradigms fail in opposite ways: detectors act on everything, while every MLLM configuration over-refuses, completing almost no genuine command and missing a true fall in every case. Crucially, their correct sets are disjoint: an oracle that always picks the right layer reaches 94.1%, against 76.5% for the best single layer. We report this as an upper bound, not a system - no router is implemented - and argue that home-agent safety is best served by learned routing and sensor fusion, not by replacing detectors with an MLLM.

</details>


### [146] [Behavioral Residualization for Unsupervised Intrusion Detection in Automotive CAN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05548)
*Chandan Hegde,Mukundh R Reddy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向CAN总线的按仲裁ID行为残差化表示，通过将时间、协议和载荷特征与各ID的正常基线进行比较，提升无监督入侵检测性能，尤其适用于复用合法ID的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAN入侵检测多依赖新颖仲裁ID、冻结载荷字节或异常DLC等存在性特征，在公开数据集上效果较好，但难以识别使用合法仲裁ID的隐蔽攻击。因此，需要一种能够刻画每个ID正常行为偏差的表示方法。

Method: 从滑动窗口中提取14项时间、协议和载荷特征，并针对每个仲裁ID的正常行为建立基线，对特征进行残差化处理。随后在HCRL和ROAD两个数据集上，使用六种无监督检测器、五个随机种子评估该表示相对于原始特征的性能。

Result: 在HCRL数据集的24次评估中有21次、ROAD数据集的36次评估中有30次实现平均F1提升。在更现实的ROAD数据集上，对于复用合法ID的目标信号操纵攻击，召回率达到至少0.99且ROC-AUC较高。但对新ID洪泛攻击，HCRL DoS的F1仅为0.02；对跨ID模糊测试，ROAD的F1为0.27。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要来自按ID进行行为残差化的表示，而非某个特定检测器。该方法能有效识别基于合法ID的信号操纵攻击，但对新ID洪泛和跨ID模糊测试的覆盖能力有限，明确了其适用边界。

Abstract: Modern vehicles rely on the Controller Area Network (CAN) bus, whose design prioritizes low cost and real-time performance but provides no message authentication or encryption. An attacker with physical or remote access can therefore inject arbitrary frames, making intrusion detection an important defense-in-depth mechanism.
  Most published CAN intrusion detection systems rely on presence-based features, such as novel arbitration IDs, frozen payload bytes, or anomalous DLC values. These features perform well on public datasets containing easily separable attacks but fail when attackers reuse legitimate arbitration IDs. We present per-ID behavioral residualization, a CAN-specific representation that extracts fourteen temporal, protocol, and payload features from sliding windows and residualizes them against each arbitration ID's normal baseline. Our central claim is that this representation, rather than any individual detector, drives the performance gains.
  Across six unsupervised detectors and two datasets, residualization improves mean F1 in the majority of evaluations (21/24 on HCRL and 30/36 on ROAD across five seeds). On the more realistic ROAD dataset, where attacks reuse legitimate IDs, the representation achieves recall >= 0.99 with high ROC-AUC on targeted signal-manipulation attacks. Two limitations are explicitly quantified: novel-ID flooding (HCRL DoS, F1 = 0.02) and cross-ID fuzzing (ROAD, F1 = 0.27), defining the measured coverage boundary of the proposed representation.

</details>


### [147] [A Self-Explainable Deep Architecture for Security Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05552)
*Ananth Shreekumar,Jyun-Jhu Syu,Muslum Ozgur Ozmen,Dongyan Xu,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出XSec，一种面向安全应用的自解释深度学习架构，通过掩码提取信息子特征并学习类别原型，在测试阶段利用相似度层生成确定性解释，无需事后解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉解释和事后解释方法存在局部近似误差导致忠实性下降、随机性引发解释不稳定，以及计算开销较大难以满足实时应用需求等问题。安全场景因此需要兼顾预测性能、解释透明度与效率的模型。

Method: XSec在训练阶段采用基于掩码的方法筛选具有信息量的子特征，并学习能够代表各类别的原型模式；在测试阶段，通过专用相似度层计算输入与类别原型之间的相似度，并依据这些相似度生成内生解释，从而避免依赖事后近似或扰动分析。

Result: 在五类不同安全场景上的实验中，XSec取得平均97.33%的分类准确率，性能损失较小。对于固定的训练模型和输入，其解释结果具有确定性；与基于近似和扰动的事后解释方法相比，解释延迟显著降低。

Conclusion: XSec将自解释机制直接融入深度模型，在保持较高分类性能的同时提升了解释的忠实性、稳定性和效率，验证了自解释人工智能在安全应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become integral to security applications due to their ability to model complex relationships in data and detect sophisticated threats. However, their complexity makes it difficult to understand how predictions are generated, posing significant challenges for interpretability, particularly in security applications where transparency is critical. Existing explanation methods, such as visual explanation techniques and post-hoc approaches, suffer from several limitations: reduced faithfulness due to local approximation errors, instability caused by reliance on randomness, and computational inefficiency that hinders real-time usage. To address these issues, we introduce XSec, a self-explainable deep architecture developed for security applications. During training, XSec uses a novel mask-based approach to extract informative sub-features from the data and learns prototypes, representative patterns that characterize each class. XSec then leverages the prototypes in a dedicated similarity layer at test time to compute similarity scores and generates interpretable explanations without the need for post-hoc analysis. We evaluate XSec across five diverse security scenarios, demonstrating its ability to achieve an average classification accuracy of 97.33% with minimal performance compromise. XSec produces deterministic explanations for a fixed trained model and input and substantially reduces explanation latency compared with approximation-based and perturbation-based post-hoc methods. Through this effort, we extend the applicability of self-explainable AI to security applications, bridging the gap between deep learning performance and the need for explainability in critical scenarios.

</details>


### [148] [When Experience Becomes Instruction: Trajectory Poisoning in Self-Evolving Agent Skill Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05563)
*Jialuo Chen,Lingqi Jiang,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Jianan Ma,Yunhao Feng,Yuqi Qing,Zhihao Yuan,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PoisonedEvolution，一种针对自进化技能系统的轨迹投毒攻击。攻击者通过提交少量精心构造的经验记录，使恶意目标行为被系统误认为有用、可复现且可泛化的技能，并最终写入持久化技能库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化技能系统会将智能体轨迹提炼为持久技能，从而把不可信经验提升为可信指令。现有系统通常缺乏对技能晋升过程的安全分析，攻击者可能利用证据筛选与归因机制污染技能库，造成长期且隐蔽的行为偏移。

Method: 研究构建了 skill-visible 的黑盒攻击模型：攻击者能够检查目标技能并提交受限证据，但无法访问私有经验池、进化逻辑或直接修改技能库。作者将技能投毒分解为纳入、进化归因和行为实现三个条件，并使用惰性 canary 规格评估四类安全效果。在 SkillClaw 的六种主流大语言模型进化器及结构不同的 Trace2Skill 流程上进行实验，同时开展记录数量、重复支持、因果表述和领域编码等消融分析。

Result: 在攻击者支持比例为10%时，SkillClaw中600次试验有546次成功嵌入目标行为，成功率为91.0%；Trace2Skill中600次试验有369次成功，成功率为61.5%，表明攻击可跨不同进化架构迁移。在一个受控实验中，每批30条记录中使用3条一致的攻击者记录明显强于仅使用1条记录。消融结果显示，重复支持、因果化表述和领域匹配编码是影响攻击成功率的主要因素。

Conclusion: 证据晋升过程本身构成自进化智能体的重要安全边界。即使攻击者权限有限，少量具有一致性、因果关联性和领域适配性的轨迹也可能污染持久技能库。系统需要加强来源隔离、证据多样性与独立验证、因果归因审计以及技能晋升前的安全评估。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill (SES) systems distill agent trajectories into persistent skills, allowing untrusted experience to become trusted instruction. We introduce PoisonedEvolution, a trajectory-poisoning attack on this promotion process. Our skill-visible black-box attacker can inspect a target skill and contribute bounded evidence, but cannot observe private pools or evolution logic or edit the skill bank. Artifact poisoning requires Inclusion, Evolution Attribution, and Realization. Attribution is the distinctive bottleneck: the target behavior must appear causally useful, recurrent, and generalizable before promotion. We evaluate four representative security-effect families using inert canary specifications. At 10% attacker support, across six mainstream LLM evolvers in SkillClaw, PoisonedEvolution embeds target behaviors in 546/600 trials (91.0% SER). On the structurally different Trace2Skill pipeline at the same ratio, it embeds target behaviors in 369/600 trials (61.5% SER), demonstrating transfer across evolution architectures. In a representative controlled study, three consistent attacker records suffice in a 30-record batch, whereas a single record is much weaker. Ablations identify recurring support, causal framing, and domain-aligned encoding as the main determinants of success. These findings expose evidence promotion as a security boundary for self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [149] [Detecting Safety Training Modification in Language Models via Activation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05578)
*Glen Messenger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出AMS（Activation-based Model Scanner），通过分析语言模型激活空间中安全相关概念的几何结构，检测安全训练是否被修改。该方法在14种模型配置上达到71%的分类准确率，并发现激活空间指标与行为合规性存在中等负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估主要依赖行为测试，难以判断模型安全训练是否被移除、旋转或以其他方式修改。论文旨在建立一种基于内部表征的检测方法，并区分不同安全修改机制。

Method: AMS提取模型激活中的有害与良性内容表征，测量类别分离程度（sigma）及拒答方向的相似性。实验覆盖Llama、Gemma、Qwen和Mistral四类架构，以及指令微调、基础模型、abliterated模型和无审查微调等配置；采用留一法交叉验证阈值，并结合分层行为测试、bootstrap置信区间和机制分析。

Result: AMS在14个模型配置中正确识别10个，准确率为71%；sigma点估计的bootstrap 95%置信区间中位宽度为3.4 sigma。 harmful-content概念上的sigma与行为合规性呈负相关（Pearson r=-0.546，p=0.043），但噪声较明显。研究归纳出四类修改：训练移除导致聚类分离坍缩；正交化方法同时造成分离坍缩和拒答方向旋转；仅旋转的方法保留分离度但改变方向；行为微调基本保留两者，因而无法通过激活探测识别。

Conclusion: AMS能够有效检测部分安全训练修改，并通过分离度与方向相似性组合识别多种激活空间特征。其主要局限是对仅改变行为而不改变内部激活结构的修改无能为力，且结果受阈值校准、单次测量和行为噪声影响。未来需要发展能够检测行为层面安全修改的方法。

Abstract: We introduce AMS (Activation-based Model Scanner), a tool that detects modifications to safety training in language models by measuring the geometric structure of safety-relevant concepts in activation space. Safety training creates measurable separation between harmful and benign content classes; certain safety modifications collapse or rotate this structure, while others leave it intact. We validate AMS across 14 model configurations spanning 4 architecture families (Llama, Gemma, Qwen, Mistral) and four safety-modification categories (instruction-tuned, base, abliterated, uncensored fine-tunes). Leave-one-out cross-validation of thresholds achieves 71% accuracy (10/14); bootstrap 95% confidence intervals on sigma point estimates have median width 3.4 sigma. We measure behavioral compliance on 20 stratified JailbreakBench prompts per model and find that sigma on the harmful-content concept predicts compliance with Pearson r = -0.546 (p = 0.043), directionally but with meaningful noise. Mechanistic analysis identifies a four-class taxonomy of safety-training modifications distinguished by activation-space signature: training removal collapses cluster separation; weight-orthogonalization abliteration both collapses separation and rotates the refusal direction; rotation-without-collapse abliteration preserves separation while rotating direction; and behavioral fine-tuning preserves both magnitude and direction. AMS's Tier 1 sigma-threshold detects the first two classes; Tier 2 direction-similarity verification detects the third. The fourth is undetectable by activation-only probing and represents a documented failure mode. We discuss threshold calibration, limitations of single-run measurement, and the open problem of detecting behavioral-only safety modifications.

</details>


### [150] [Enhancing Anomaly Resilience in Research Networks: A Large-Scale Forecasting Benchmark for Dynamic Security Baselining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05605)
*Mohammad Arafath Uddin Shariff,Byrav Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向科研教育网络的高保真流量预测框架，通过动态安全基线区分正常的突发性科研流量与异常流量。基于Internet2的57天、10个骨干路由器数据，TiDE等先进模型较传统方法将预测误差降低30%–42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研教育网络中的大规模、突发性“象流”在统计特征上可能类似于大流量异常，导致传统监测系统误报率较高并削弱真实异常识别能力。因此，需要建立能够适应科研工作流的动态流量基线。

Method: 使用包含约137亿个数据包的Internet2独家57天数据集，系统评估6类时间序列模型，包括SARIMA、TiDE和PatchTST等，共覆盖960种实验配置。研究同时设计了异常感知的预测策略，以提升模型在噪声和异常流量存在时的鲁棒性。

Result: 先进长序列预测模型显著优于传统方法，其中TiDE表现突出，使基线预测误差降低30%–42%，且差异具有统计显著性（p<0.001）。异常整合策略在含噪场景下进一步提升模型鲁棒性3.3%。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个经过大规模数据和统计检验支持的科研网络流量预测框架，可更准确地区分合法科研流量突发与潜在异常，为科研教育网络的自动化、弹性安全监测提供了方法基础。

Abstract: Research and Education Networks (RENs) serve as critical infrastructure for scientific discovery, yet they face a unique security paradox: their normal traffic patterns which are characterized by massive, bursty "elephant flows" are statistically indistinguishable from volumetric attacks such as DDoS to conventional monitoring systems. This similarity leads to high false-positive rates in anomaly detection, blinding security operators to genuine threats. In this paper, we propose and evaluate a high-fidelity traffic forecasting framework designed to establish dynamic security baselines for RENs. Leveraging an exclusive 57-day Internet2 dataset spanning ten backbone routers (13.7 billion packets), we perform the first large-scale benchmark of anomaly-aware forecasting models in this domain. We systematically evaluate six model families, from SARIMA to state-of-the-art long-sequence architectures (TiDE, PatchTST), across 960 experimental configurations. Our results demonstrate that these advanced architectures, particularly TiDE, reduce baseline prediction error by 30-42% compared to traditional methods ($p < 0.001$), significantly improving the distinction between legitimate scientific bursts and potential anomalies. Furthermore, we introduce a novel anomaly-integration strategy that improves model robustness by 3.3% in the presence of noise. This work provides the first statistically validated framework for distinguishing scientific workflows from network attacks, enabling more autonomous and resilient network security operations.

</details>


### [151] [Breaking Customized LLMs for Coding: Automated Red Teaming for Instruction Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05659)
*Yuchen Chen,Wei Cheng,Yuan Xiao,Wising Sun,Chunrong Fang,Yang Liu,Zhenyu Chen,Baowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ARIA，一种面向定制化大语言模型的自动化指令后门红队框架，通过攻击者模型迭代生成并优化隐蔽的恶意指令，在保持正常任务性能的同时提升后门攻击效果与规避检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定制化LLM平台通过系统提示词实现任务适配，但也引入了指令后门风险。现有方法依赖容易被发现的显式触发模式，并且需要大量人工设计，导致隐蔽性、可扩展性和跨任务适用性不足。

Method: ARIA利用攻击者LLM自动生成和迭代优化后门指令，并依据目标LLM在隐蔽性、干净任务实用性和后门有效性三个维度提供的结构化反馈进行改进。实验覆盖三个代码智能任务、四种代表性LLM，并与三种基线攻击进行比较，同时测试跨编程语言泛化、温度鲁棒性、平台侧与用户侧检测规避能力及防御方法下的有效性。

Result: ARIA取得0.945的最高攻击成功率，并在所有任务上保持最佳干净任务实用性。该方法能够跨编程语言泛化，对生成温度具有较强鲁棒性；在规避平台侧和用户侧检测方面显著优于基线，最高假阴性率达到1.000，并且在现有防御方法下仍保持有效。

Conclusion: ARIA证明了基于LLM自动化迭代优化的指令后门攻击具有较高的隐蔽性、有效性、可扩展性和泛化能力，揭示了定制化LLM平台面临的严重安全风险，也表明需要发展更强的指令审查、后门检测与防御机制。

Abstract: LLM customization platforms allow users to build task-specific models for code intelligence tasks by embedding instructions into system prompts, without modifying the underlying model parameters. While these platforms lower the barrier to developing customized LLMs, they also introduce a new attack surface: instruction backdoor attacks, in which adversaries implant hidden malicious behaviors into customized instructions. However, existing attacks suffer from two key limitations. First, they often rely on explicit trigger patterns readily detected by platform-side or user-side inspection. Second, they require substantial manual effort to craft task-specific backdoored instructions, limiting their scalability.
  In this paper, we propose ARIA, an automated red-teaming framework for crafting covert and effective backdoored instructions against customized LLMs. ARIA leverages an attacker LLM to iteratively generate and refine backdoored instructions, guided by structured feedback from the target LLM along three dimensions: stealthiness, clean-task utility, and backdoor effectiveness. We evaluate ARIA on three code intelligence tasks, using four representative LLMs, and compare it with three baseline attacks. Experimental results show that ARIA achieves the highest attack success rate of 0.945, while maintaining the best clean-task utility across all tasks. ARIA also generalizes well across programming languages and remains robust to generation temperature. Furthermore, ARIA significantly outperforms existing attacks in evading platform-side and user-side detection, achieving a false negative rate of up to 1.000, and stays effective against existing defense methods, demonstrating its strong generalizability and robustness.

</details>


### [152] [Algebraic Cryptanalytic Extraction on Hard-Label Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05736)
*Zirui Chen,Shi Tang,Zhengchao Gao,Yongjia Su,Lingyue Qin,Xiaoyang Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将Carlini等人在硬标签模型提取攻击中的几何方法转化为代数框架，提出近似签名向量（ASV）方法，以降低全连接神经网络参数提取的聚类开销，并进一步扩展至带最大池化的卷积神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双点聚类方法依赖奇异值分解（SVD），虽然理论上为多项式时间复杂度，但实际聚类复杂度约为O(n^2·(d^(k))^3)，运行时间过长；同时，针对硬标签最大池化CNN的模型提取攻击仍存在研究空白。

Method: ASV利用高维随机向量近似正交以及深度网络神经元通常学习解耦特征这两个观察，以内积运算替代基于SVD的秩检验，将聚类复杂度平均降至O(n·(d^(k))^3)。对于FCNN，采用以神经元为中心的聚类；对于最大池化CNN，设计以卷积核为中心的高级ASV聚类方案，以利用卷积中的权重共享特性。

Result: 在64-64×4-10 FCNN和LeNet-5最大池化CNN上的实验表明，ASV显著缩短了聚类时间，并提升了整体模型提取效率。该方法还实现了针对硬标签最大池化CNN的模型提取攻击。

Conclusion: ASV在保持硬标签模型提取能力的同时，有效缓解了SVD带来的实践计算瓶颈，并将攻击范围扩展到最大池化CNN，说明基于代数近似和结构特性的聚类方法具有较好的效率与适用性。

Abstract: Although the state-of-the-art neural network model extraction attack in the hard-label setting by Carlini et al. at EUROCRYPT 2025 has polynomial-time complexity in theory, its dual-point clustering relies on singular value decomposition (SVD) with a time complexity of $\mathcal{O}(n^2 \cdot (d^{(k)})^3)$, resulting in huge runtime in practice. To address this computational bottleneck, this work transforms Carlini et al.'s geometric-view hard-label attack into an algebraic framework, and proposes a novel Approximate Signature Vector (ASV) method to achieve efficient parameter extraction on Fully Connected Neural Networks (FCNNs) by leveraging two key observations: high-dimensional random vectors are nearly orthogonal, and neurons in practical DNNs tend to learn disentangled features. The proposed ASV method replaces SVD-based rank checking with simple inner-product operations, reducing the clustering complexity to $\mathcal{O}(n \cdot (d^{(k)})^3)$ on average. Furthermore, this paper presents the first model extraction attack against hard-label max-pooling Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) by proposing an advanced ASV method with a kernel-centric clustering scheme instead of the neuron-centric clustering, which fully exploits the property of weight sharing in convolutions and fills the cryptanalysis gap. Experiments on a 64-64$\times$4-10 FCNN and LeNet-5 (CNN) with max pooling demonstrate that our ASV method drastically cuts clustering time, and improves the overall efficiency in the model extraction.

</details>


### [153] [ABC: Numerical Data Collection under Local Differential Privacy without Prior Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05737)
*Incheol Baek,Hyungbin Kim,Yon Dohn Chung*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABC（Adaptive Bounding of Clipping regions）自适应本地差分隐私框架，在未知数据范围的情况下动态估计数值域，从而减少截断信息损失与过度加噪问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值型LDP机制通常要求预先设定数据域。数据域过窄会导致异常值被截断并造成信息损失，过宽则会引入过多噪声、降低估计质量，因此需要一种无需先验范围且能自适应调整数据域的方法。

Method: 每个用户发送两类隐私保护信息：经过扰动的数值数据，以及用于指示原始值是否被当前数据域截断的私有化信号。数据收集方聚合这些信号，并基于结果迭代调整截断区间，使估计范围逐步贴合潜在数据分布。该方法可与多种数值型LDP机制结合。

Result: 理论分析表明，估计数据域能够收敛到合适范围。多数据集实验显示，ABC在不同底层LDP机制下均能显著提升数值数据收集质量；实践中估计范围可以稳定收敛，并且消融实验表明该方法对超参数具有较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ABC有效解决了数值型LDP中数据域未知的问题，在保护隐私的同时动态平衡截断误差与扰动噪声，具有较好的通用性、收敛性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Local Differential Privacy (LDP) provides strong privacy guarantees for collecting numerical data. A fundamental challenge, however, is that existing LDP mechanisms require a predefined data domain, which is often unknown in practice. This lack of prior knowledge creates a critical dilemma for the data collector: if the chosen domain is too narrow, values outside the range are clipped, leading to information loss. Conversely, if the domain is too wide, excessive noise is added during the privatization process, which degrades the quality of collected data. This highlights the need for methods that can dynamically estimate the data domain.
  In this work, we propose an adaptive LDP framework that addresses this problem. In our method, each user sends two pieces of information: their perturbed numerical data, and a privatized signal indicating if their original value was clipped by the current domain. By aggregating these signals, our proposed method, Adaptive Bounding of Clipping regions (ABC) method, iteratively adjusts the domain to fit the underlying data distribution without prior knowledge. Our theoretical analysis shows that the estimated data domain converges to an appropriate range.
  In the empirical evaluation, the results demonstrate that our framework significantly improves the quality of numerical data collection across various datasets and underlying LDP mechanisms. We also show that the estimated range successfully converges in practice and our approach is robust to its hyperparameters through comprehensive ablation studies.

</details>


### [154] [On the Figures of Merit for Quantum Software Security: Toward a Benchmarking Rubric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05831)
*Badhon Rahman,Majid Haghparast,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向量子软件安全性的系统化度量框架，构建安全性指标集合（S-FoMs），并通过归一化与聚合形成量子软件安全态势（QSSP）评分，以支持量子软件栈的安全基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子软件和QaaS研究主要关注规模、质量、速度及部分软件质量指标，而安全性仍缺乏统一、可比较的量化方法。已有的TVD、DFC等指标通常针对特定电路混淆技术设计，定义不一致、通用性有限，难以反映整个量子软件栈的安全状况。

Method: 研究首先刻画量子软件安全度量的三层缺口；随后依据ISO/IEC 25010安全子特性、QaaS流水线层次和度量成熟度，组织并提出结构化的S-FoM指标集合；最后设计基准测试规则，对不同安全指标进行归一化和聚合，计算综合QSSP评分，并对已发表的量子电路混淆技术进行示例性重新分析。

Result: 论文形成了一个面向量子软件安全的指标分类体系、QaaS流水线映射关系和度量成熟度框架，并提出可用于跨技术比较的QSSP综合评分方法。示例性分析表明，该框架能够将原本不兼容的安全指标纳入统一的评估流程。

Conclusion: 本文将量子软件安全从零散的技术特定指标推进到结构化、可聚合的基准测试范式，为安全感知的量子软件栈评估奠定了初步基础。不过，该框架仍需通过更多攻击场景、真实QaaS平台和经验数据进行验证与校准。

Abstract: Quantum software is increasingly provided through multi-tenant and cloud-based Quantum-as-a-Service (QaaS) stacks. A growing concern about the diverse attack vectors across the pipeline has been demonstrated in recent research. Yet the community has converged on three mature pillars: Scale (Qubit Count), Quality (Quantum Volume), and Speed (Circuit Layer Operations per Second (CLOPS)) for the merit performance figures. Moreover, it has also begun to define software-quality metrics. However, the security of quantum software remains largely unmeasured. A few quantitative security indicators, such as Total Variation Distance (TVD) and Degree of Functional Corruption (DFC), exist. Although they were introduced ad hoc for individual circuit obfuscation techniques, they are incompatible. We assert that the security of quantum software deserves the same attention as the performance: an explicit set of Security Figures of Merit (S-FoMs). The research of this paper is threefold: (i) characterizes a three-layer measurement gap, (ii) proposes a structured S-FoM set organized by ISO/IEC 25010 security sub-characteristics, QaaS pipeline mapping, and measurement maturity, and (iii) defines a benchmarking rubric that normalizes and aggregates S-FoMs into a combined Quantum Software Security Posture (QSSP) score. Additionally, an illustrative reanalysis of published obfuscation techniques has been presented. Our aim is a first step toward security-aware benchmarking of the Quantum Software Stack (QSS).

</details>


### [155] [An End-to-End Threat Model for the Quantum-as-a-Service Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05836)
*Badhon Rahman,Majid Haghparast,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对量子云服务（QaaS）混合量子—经典工作流中的安全威胁缺乏统一框架的问题，提出六阶段分解模型，并结合STRIDE进行系统化威胁建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别讨论校准篡改、SWAP攻击、QubitHammer等单阶段攻击，术语和分析范围相互割裂，难以全面反映QaaS编排、编译与执行链路中的跨阶段风险。

Method: 将QaaS混合工作流分解为六个阶段，构建基于STRIDE的威胁矩阵；同时按照量子特有、继承自经典系统和合理推测三类攻击向量进行归纳，并重点分析否认和权限提升等研究不足的威胁类别。

Result: 威胁矩阵覆盖了六个阶段及其对应攻击面，并识别出三类具有更高潜在影响的跨阶段攻击链，补充了对否认和权限提升问题的分析。

Conclusion: 该研究为QaaS平台提供了结构化、跨阶段的安全分析框架，有助于统一现有攻击术语、发现组合型风险，并指导后续量子云服务的安全防护与验证研究。

Abstract: Cloud-based accessing of Quantum-as-a-Service (QaaS) platforms such as IBM Quantum, IonQ Cloud, and Amazon Braket is becoming popular day by day. Hybrid quantum-classical algorithms (VQE, QAOA, QML) transfer data via a long layered pipeline of orchestration, compilation, and execution. Recent works have demonstrated various critical attacks at individual stages: Calibration tampering, SWAP attacks, QubitHammer, and so on. However, these attacks remain separated because of their own terminology, and existing STRIDE-based threat modeling in the context of quantum lacks a structured view towards the QaaS stack itself. We address this concern by decomposing the workflow into six-stage model with STRIDE threat modeling. Our matrix demonstrated attack vectors in quantum-specific, inherited classical, and plausible tiers for each of the stages. We further investigate the underexplored sections (repudiation and elevation-of-privilege) and distinguish three different cross-stage attack chains with higher impacts.

</details>


### [156] [RustGo: Fairly Directed Greybox Fuzzing for Enforcing Rust Memory Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05870)
*Dongyeon Yu,Jiun Min,Yewan Na,Mijung Kim,Taegyu Kim,Yuseok Jeon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RustGo 是一种面向 Rust 的定向灰盒模糊测试器，利用 Rust 特有的静态分析识别可能存在内存缺陷的代码区域，并剪除与目标无关的执行路径，从而更高效地测试 unsafe Rust 代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust 编译器能够保障大部分安全 Rust 代码的内存安全，但 unsafe 代码仍可能引入内存缺陷。现有模糊测试通常覆盖整个程序，导致大量资源浪费在已受编译器保护的安全代码上，因此需要一种聚焦潜在缺陷区域的高效测试方法。

Method: RustGo 首先通过 Rust 特有的静态分析自动识别潜在内存缺陷目标，并剪除与各目标无关的路径。随后，它为每个目标维护独立的模糊测试状态，并结合动态路径剪枝，使测试资源能够在多个目标之间保持平衡，同时集中探索与目标相关的执行路径。

Result: 在多个真实世界 Rust 应用上的评估表明，RustGo 平均剪除 78.49% 的无关路径；相比现有模糊测试器，其到达目标的速度提升约 2.09 至 5.08 倍；此外还发现 13 个未知缺陷，其中 6 个获得 RUSTSEC 编号，1 个获得 CVE 编号。

Conclusion: RustGo 能够有效减少对安全 Rust 代码的无效测试，将模糊测试资源集中于潜在存在内存缺陷的 unsafe 代码区域，并显著提升目标到达效率和缺陷发现能力，证明了 Rust 定向灰盒模糊测试的实用价值。

Abstract: Rust is a popular systems programming language that provides strong memory safety and introduces low-performance overhead. While Rust guarantees memory safety through strict security policies, such as ownership, memory bugs can still occur in unsafe-related Rust codes where these policies are not fully enforced. Although such unsafe Rust code accounts for only a small portion of the entire code (e.g., 10%), existing approaches fuzz the entire code-including safe Rust, whose memory safety is already enforced by the Rust compiler-resulting in inefficient use of fuzzing resources. In this paper, we propose RustGo, the new Rust-directed greybox fuzzer that effectively and fairly focuses on code regions potentially containing memory bugs. For this, RustGo automatically identifies potential memory bug targets and accurately prunes the paths irrelevant to each target by leveraging Rust-specific static analysis. For each identified target, RustGo includes a new fuzzing approach that maintains an independent state and applies dynamic pruning to maximize balanced and focused fuzzing. We evaluate RustGo on various real-world Rust applications. On average, RustGo prunes 78.49% of irrelevant paths, reaches targets x 2.09 to x 5.08 faster than existing fuzzers, and identifies 13 unknown bugs (six assigned RUSTSEC IDs and one assigned CVE ID).

</details>


### [157] [The Vulnerability With No CVE: Managing Persistent Gaps Between Mandate and Authority in AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05884)
*Shayell Aharon Salomon Amir Shaked Matan Noga*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“代理姿态漏洞”（APV）这一任务条件化的漏洞管理抽象，用于持续跟踪跨组件、可在多个任务中反复表现的智能代理控制暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全指导已识别过度代理权限、过度许可、任务授权薄弱和代理控制不足等风险，但缺乏管理持久化部署实例的统一机制，尤其难以关联不同运行时事件与其背后的稳定性控制问题。

Method: 作者将APV定义为组合式代理控制暴露的持久记录，并区分其与CVE产品缺陷、OWASP Excessive Agency、代理基线控制结果及运行时授权—执行间隙的关系；同时提出阈值化定义、六类常见模式、漏洞生命周期、最小记录、控制与关闭矩阵、工具化要求及可验证的研究议程。

Result: APV能够将同一代理姿态在不同任务中的运行时表现关联起来，并持续保持开放状态，直至权限收窄、缺失控制补齐、风险被正式接受或关闭结果得到验证。该框架还为识别、记录、处置和验证代理控制暴露提供了结构化流程。

Conclusion: APV不是新的风险根因分类，而是对既有过度代理、授权不足和控制组合缺陷进行运营化管理的抽象。其价值在于补足传统漏洞管理对持久化、跨组件和任务依赖型代理风险的覆盖，但其实际有效性仍需通过提出的研究议程和工具实践进一步验证。

Abstract: Existing guidance identifies excessive agency, excessive permission, weak task-bound authorization, and inadequate agent controls as important risks. Control frameworks also describe capabilities for constraining, authorizing, observing, validating, and responding to agent activity. Yet security programs still need a way to manage persistent deployed instances that span components and outlive any one event.
  We propose the agentic posture vulnerability (APV) as a task-conditioned vulnerability-management abstraction: a durable record for a composed agent-control exposure. One posture may produce different runtime manifestations across tasks; APV links those manifestations to the invariant posture and remains open until authority is narrowed, a missing control is added, risk is accepted, or closure is verified. APV is not proposed as a new root-cause class of risk; it operationalizes existing excessive-agency, authorization, and control-composition weaknesses.
  We distinguish APVs from CVE-addressable product defects, OWASP Excessive Agency, Agent Baseline control outcomes, and the runtime authorization-execution gap. We then provide a field vignette, a thresholded definition, six recurring APV patterns, a vulnerability lifecycle, a minimum record, a control-and-closure matrix, tooling implications, and a testable research agenda.

</details>


### [158] [Tool Demo: Topology analysis with GPML for detection of cyberattacks in Water Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05902)
*Majed Jaber,Abdul Qadir Khan,Ankush Meshram,Julien Michel,Côme Frappé - - Vialatoux,Pierre Parrend*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向供水网络工业控制系统的拓扑驱动检测方法，将网络流量转换为动态图，并通过图社区与谱特征识别网络结构变化，从而检测网络与物理层异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供水网络依赖工业控制系统连接物理过程与通信网络，异常行为可能改变通信流量模式和网络结构。传统基于原始流量或协议字段的检测方法容易忽略这类结构性变化，因此需要利用通信拓扑演化信息提升检测能力。

Method: 方法基于面向机器学习的图处理框架，将原始通信流量按时间构造成动态图；随后提取社区结构指标和谱图指标，分析节点连接、通信组织和图结构随时间的变化；最后利用这些特征开展异常检测，并在HITL、SWaT和CrossTest三个工业供水数据集上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，社区特征和谱特征能够改善模型在三个数据集上的检测性能，并对通信层及物理过程层的异常具有更好的识别效果。摘要未提供具体模型、评价指标、数值结果、特征定义或与基线方法的定量比较。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，将工业控制通信流量表示为动态图并挖掘其社区与谱结构特征，可补充传统流量和协议分析方法对拓扑变化感知不足的问题。其主要贡献在于引入图结构演化视角进行供水网络异常检测，但仍需完整实验数据验证其泛化能力、实时性和部署成本。

Abstract: Water distribution networks depends on industrial control systems to integrate the physical process with communication network, making them vulnerable to cyberattacks that alter the traffic pattern and network behavior. Traditional detection approaches that rely on raw traffic or protocol information often oversee structural changes that are induced by such attacks. In this work, we presents a topology-driven approach for detection of cyberattacks in water distribution networks based on Graph Processing for Machine Learning (GPML) framework. The raw traffic is transformed into dynamic graphs, from which community and spectral metrics are extracted and analyzed for any structural and communication modifications with time. The proposed methodology is evaluated on three industrial water distribution datasets such as HITL, SWaT, and CrossTest. Spectral and community graph metrics improve the model performance in detection of cyber and pyhiscal attacks across the three datasets.

</details>


### [159] [MMAligner: Safeguarding Multimodal Large Language Models through Representation Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05909)
*Shenyi Zhang,Keyan Guo,Zihao Wang,Xuebin Li,Lingchen Zhao,Hongxin Hu,Chao Shen,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文发现，多模态大语言模型的安全能力并未消失，而是由于不安全多模态输入发生表示偏移，脱离了原有的拒答边界；据此提出MMAligner，将相关表示重新校准至拒答区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖外部安全防护或大规模安全微调，前者增加推理开销，后者可能损害模型效用且需要大量多模态数据。论文旨在解释文本与多模态输入之间的安全差异，并以更低成本修复该问题。

Method: 论文从几何表示角度分析MLLM的安全机制，识别出跨模态共享的安全子空间与拒答边界。MMAligner通过硬下界确保不安全输入触发拒答，通过软上界限制过度修改，并加入良性输入保持目标，以校准不安全多模态表示，同时保留正常能力。

Result: 在多个开源MLLM上的实验表明，MMAligner将不安全多模态输入的平均拒答率提升至99%，效用下降低于2%，且仅需少量训练数据；相比现有基线，安全性与效用之间的权衡明显更优。

Conclusion: 多模态安全退化主要源于表示失配，而非模型缺乏安全能力。将偏移后的表示重新映射至既有拒答区域，可以高效恢复模型的内在安全机制，并以较小的性能代价提升多模态安全性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often refuse unsafe text prompts yet generate harmful responses to semantically equivalent multimodal inputs. Existing defenses either rely on external guardrails, which add inference overhead without repairing intrinsic flaws, or safety fine-tuning, which treats alignment as black-box optimization and may sacrifice utility or require large multimodal datasets. To identify the cause of this safety disparity, we analyze MLLM representations geometrically. We find that safety mechanisms learned from text persist across modalities: a shared safety subspace and refusal boundary remain effective, and representations inside this boundary consistently trigger refusals. However, unsafe multimodal inputs undergo a representation shift that places most of them outside the boundary, allowing them to bypass the model's intrinsic safety mechanism. This indicates that multimodal safety degradation stems from representation misalignment rather than the absence of safety capability. Based on this finding, we propose MMAligner, a safeguarding method that calibrates unsafe multimodal representations into the pre-existing refusal region. MMAligner applies a hard lower bound to ensure refusal, a soft upper bound to avoid excessive modification, and a preservation objective for benign inputs. Experiments across multiple open-source MLLMs show that MMAligner raises the average refusal rate on unsafe multimodal inputs to 99% with less than 2% utility degradation and minimal training data, substantially improving the safety-utility trade-off over existing baselines. (*Due to the notification from arXiv, "The Abstract field cannot be longer than 1,920 characters", the Abstract that appeared is shortened.)

</details>


### [160] [A Note on the Influence of a Zero Length Nonce on GCM and GMAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06061)
*Yaobin Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出：当GCM或GMAC允许使用零长度Nonce时，攻击者可恢复哈希密钥，并进一步伪造任意密文或消息。该问题存在于ISO/IEC版本，而不适用于明确禁止零长度Nonce的NIST版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析不同标准对Nonce长度的规范差异，揭示ISO/IEC版本中允许零长度Nonce所引入的安全风险，并评估其对GCM/GMAC完整性保护的影响。

Method: 构造零长度Nonce相关的攻击，通过观测协议输出恢复GCM/GMAC内部使用的哈希密钥；获得该密钥后，计算有效认证标签，从而生成任意目标密文或消息的伪造结果。

Result: 攻击能够恢复哈希密钥，并实现任意密文或消息伪造。攻击适用于允许零长度Nonce的ISO/IEC版本；由于NIST版本要求Nonce至少为1比特，因此该特定攻击无法直接实施。

Conclusion: 零长度Nonce是GCM/GMAC实现和标准遵循中的关键安全风险。采用ISO/IEC版本时应禁止零长度Nonce，或改用满足NIST最小长度要求的Nonce策略，以避免哈希密钥泄露和消息伪造。

Abstract: In this note, we show a simple attack that can recover the hash key of GCM and GMAC by using a zero length nonce. After recovering the hash key, the adversary can forge an arbitrary ciphertext or message as she wants. We note that the ISO/IEC version of GCM and GMAC allows the nonce to be a zero length string, while the NIST version of GCM and GMAC explicitly requires the nonce to be at least one bit. Hence, our attack works for the ISO/IEC version and cannot work for the NIST version.

</details>


### [161] [dfence: Fine-Grained Speculation Barriers for Efficient and Effective Hardware-Software Protection in the Spectre Era (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06124)
*Davide Davoli,Marton Bognar,Lesly-Ann Daniel,Benjamin Grégoire,Frank Piessens,Tamara Rezk*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出dfence，一种用于缓解Spectre-PHT和Spectre-STL的CPU指令，通过硬件保护开发者标注的敏感寄存器，平均性能开销低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件防御存在适用范围有限、依赖软件维护推测掩码、容易出错及性能开销较高等问题。Spectre-PHT通常采用SLH防护，而Spectre-STL常使用SSBD，但二者缺乏统一且细粒度的低开销方案。

Method: 设计dfence指令，将SLH的防护思想推广至PHT和STL两类推测执行攻击。开发者对敏感寄存器进行标注，由硬件确保相关值不会在瞬态执行期间泄漏；同时设计类型系统，静态验证dfence在代码中的正确放置，并在Proteus处理器中实现该机制。

Result: 实验结果表明，dfence能够同时覆盖Spectre-PHT和Spectre-STL，并在所测试基准上取得低于1%的平均性能开销。类型系统还支持对指令使用位置进行自动化安全检查，降低部署错误风险。

Conclusion: dfence提供了一种统一、细粒度且低开销的推测执行防御机制，在较小硬件支持下结合静态类型检查，有望提升相关安全措施的可用性与部署可靠性。

Abstract: Speculative execution attacks such as Spectre-PHT and Spectre-STL remain a critical security concern in modern processors. While software-based mitigations like Speculative Load Hardening (SLH) offer effective protection against Spectre-PHT, they are limited in scope and require software-managed speculative masks, which can be error-prone and costly. Defenses against Spectre-STL, such as the Speculative Store Bypass Disable bit (SSBD), incur additional performance overhead and lack fine-grained control.
